- **see-also**: Generate Wikipedia-style See Also sections — austere lists of kin objects, concepts, texts, practices, or phenomena adjacent to a given subject without arguing or explaining the kinship. The reader performs the binding. Default deployment is reflexive: produce the See Also for *this conversation* — what would appear at the bottom of the Wikipedia article our chat would constitute. Form rules: bullet list, 5-15 entries, no editorial gloss (brief disambiguating parenthetical only when truly needed), no thesis, mix of obvious and slightly-oblique entries. The slightly-strange entry is load-beari… ([Wondermonger-daydreaming/claude-skills-library](https://github.com/Wondermonger-daydreaming/claude-skills-library/tree/HEAD/skills/see-also) · ★6)
- **see-also-docs**: Create or refresh `docs/RELATED_PROJECTS.md` as a visitor guide to comparable projects and resources, alternatives, prior art, lineage, and closely related companion work. ([vosslab/vosslab-skills](https://github.com/vosslab/vosslab-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/documentation/see-also-docs) · ★2)
- **see-it-first**: Forces reproduction before any fix. When something doesn't work, this skill stops the symptom→guess→patch loop and first reproduces the bug locally on the same branch, data, and inputs the user saw — because an unreproduced bug is an unsolved one. 버그·이상 동작 앞에서 추측 패치의 유혹을 끊고, 먼저 눈앞에 똑같이 재현하게 만드는 엔지니어링 철학 스킬. ([kwakseongjae/some-engineer-mind](https://github.com/kwakseongjae/some-engineer-mind/tree/HEAD/see-it-first) · ★1)
- **see-it-in-figma**: Prototype a design problem in Figma against Cornerstone's real variables and components, before anyone commits to an implementation. Use when someone wants to see, sketch, mock up, try, or compare an interface idea — a state that has no component yet, an empty or error state, a layout or density question, a variant that may not be needed — or wants to check whether an idea holds up across Cru and FamilyLife in light and dark. Figma only — it never writes application code. ([CruGlobal/cornerstone-design-system](https://github.com/CruGlobal/cornerstone-design-system/tree/HEAD/plugins/cornerstone-designer/skills/see-it-in-figma))
- **see-it-live**: Launch the app and confirm the specific change is visible and working: screenshot, curl, or run it. Doubles as the fast smoke pass. Use for run the app, start the server, see it live, smoke test, or post-deploy check. ([alex-macra/claude-codex-skills-assembly](https://github.com/alex-macra/claude-codex-skills-assembly/tree/HEAD/skills/see-it-live) · ★1)
- **see-through-anime-layer-decomposition**: Expertise in See-through, a framework for single-image layer decomposition of anime characters into manipulatable 2.5D PSD files using diffusion models.
